Usage notes:
- If there's already a CLAUDE.md, suggest improvements to it.
- When you make the initial CLAUDE.md, do not repeat yourself and do not include obvious instructions like "Provide helpful error messages to users", "Write unit tests for all new utilities", "Never include sensitive information (API keys, tokens) in code or commits".

- Don't include generic development practices.

Keep commits small and avoid checking in large binaries.\n     4→\n     5→## Project Structure & Module Organization\n     6→\n     7→- `repos/vllm-omni/`: primary Python repo (library + CLI). Code under `repos/vllm-omni/vllm_omni/`, tests under `repos/vllm-omni/tests/`.\n     8→- `repos/sglang/`: local clone of SGLang (used here for `sglang[diffusion]` experiments; current work uses branch `feat/svdquant`).\n     9→- `workspace/experiments/`: benchmark scripts (e.g. diffusers vs sglang).\n    10→- `workspace/third_party/`: third-party integrations (e.g. ComfyUI variants).\n    11→- `workspace/docs/`: notes, reports, handoff docs.\n    12→- `data/models/`: local model folders.\n    13→- `data/weights/`: **shared weight store** for `.safetensors` (do not commit).\n    14→- `data/test_sets/`: small image fixtures for repeatable runs.\n    15→- `artifacts/`: benchmark outputs and bundles.\n    16→- `.hf_cache/`: HuggingFace cache (legacy location; large; do not commit).\n    17→\n    18→## Build, Test, and Development Commands\n    19→\n    20→From this workspace user_4813494d:\n    21→\n    22→```bash\n    23→# Run diffusers+nunchaku baseline benchmark\n    24→conda run -p .conda/envs/nunchaku-base \\\n    25→  python workspace/experiments/bench_diffusers_qwen_edit.py \\\n    26→  --quantized-transformer data/weights/nunchaku_qwen_image_edit_2511_ultimate_speed_int4.safetensors \\\n    27→  --lora data/weights/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\n    28→\n    29→# SGLang diffusion CLI (after installing sglang[diffusion])\n    30→conda run -p .conda/envs/sglang-diff \\\n    31→  sglang generate --model-path Qwen/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 --help\n    32→```\n    33→\n    34→## Coding Style & Naming Conventions\n    35→\n    36→- Python: 4-space indentation, `snake_case` for functions/modules.\n    37→- Prefer repository tooling over ad-hoc formatting.\n    38→  - `repos/vllm-omni/` uses `pre-commit` and typically a Ruff-based workflow.\n    39→\n    40→## Testing Guidelines\n    41→\n    42→- `repos/vllm-omni/` uses `pytest`. Example:\n    43→\n    44→```bash\n    45→cd repos/vllm-omni && pytest -q\n    46→```\n    47→\n    48→- Add tests near the touched area (follow existing `test_*.py` naming).\n    49→\n    50→## Commit & Pull Request Guidelines\n    51→\n    52→- Recent `repos/vllm-omni/` history commonly uses short **bracketed prefixes** (e.g. `[Bugfix]`, `[Doc]`, `[Perf]`) and links PRs/issues via `(#1234)`.\n    53→- PRs should include: what changed, how to reproduce, and performance numbers when relevant (especially for diffusion/serving).\n    54→\n    55→## Agent-Specific Notes\n    56→\n    57→- Don’t create new “rules/*” meta files; keep guidance in `AGENTS.md`.\n    58→- Don’t end responses with leading prompts like “如果你还想…/If you want…”.\n    59→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. {"text": "1→# CLAUDE.md\n     2→\n     3→This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.\n     4→\n     5→## Project Overview\n     6→\n     7→This repository manages ComfyUI Docker image deployment for the Infini-AI (无问芯穹) platform. The workflow involves building custom Docker images with specific ComfyUI plugins, testing them locally, and deploying them to the platform for use in web-based workflows.\n     8→\n     9→## Environment Architecture\n    10→\n    11→### Directory Structure\n    12→- `/mnt/public/` - Public models and shared files (read-only resources)\n    13→- `/mnt/private/` - Private workspace and models\n    14→  - `/mnt/private/models/` - Custom models organized by type:\n    15→    - `diffusion_models/` - Main diffusion models\n    16→    - `text_encoders/` - Text encoder models\n    17→    - `vae/` - VAE models\n    18→    - `loras/` - LoRA models\n    19→  - `/mnt/private/workspace/` - Working directory for scripts and files\n    20→  - `/mnt/private/workspace/Dockerfile` - Main Dockerfile to maintain\n    21→\n    22→### Base Image\n    23→- Uses `harbor.infini-ai.com/infini-maas/comfyui-base:comfyui-0.3.73-torch2.7.0-cuda12.8-cudnn9`\n    24→- Python 3.11, PyTorch 2.7.0, CUDA 12.8, cuDNN 9\n    25→\n    26→## Common Commands\n    27→\n    28→### Proxy Configuration\n    29→Always set proxy before operations requiring external network access:\n    30→```bash\n    31→export http_proxy=http://10.12.4.10:1089\n    32→export https_proxy=http://10.12.4.10:1089\n    33→```\n    34→\n    35→### Building Docker Image\n    36→Build with proxy for external dependencies:\n    37→```bash\n    38→docker build \\\n    39→  --build-arg=\"HTTP_PROXY=http://10.12.4.10:1089\" \\\n    40→  --build-arg=\"HTTPS_PROXY=http://10.12.4.10:1089\" \\\n    41→  -t demo-comfyui .\n    42→```\n    43→\n    44→### Running Docker Container for Testing\n    45→Enter container with GPU access and mounts:\n    46→```bash\n    47→docker run --gpus all --rm -ti -p 7860:7860 -v /mnt:/mnt --entrypoint bash demo-comfyui\n    48→```\n    49→\n    50→Start ComfyUI inside container:\n    51→```bash\n    52→bash /mnt/public/scripts/entrypoints.sh\n    53→```\n    54→\n    55→### Port Forwarding for Testing\n    56→Forward ComfyUI port to access from local machine:\n    57→```bash\n    58→ssh -L 7860:127.0.0.1:7860 -p 41531 [REDACTED]\n    59→```\n    60→Then access at: `http://127.0.0.1:7860/`\n    61→\n    62→### Deploying to Platform\n    63→Push image to platform (requires API key):\n    64→```bash\n    65→export [REDACTED]\n    66→python3 /mnt/public/scripts/build_comfy_env.py <image_id>\n    67→```\n    68→\n    69→### Downloading Models\n    70→Use the `download_z_image_turbo.sh` script to download models via aria2c with proxy:\n    71→```bash\n    72→./download_z_image_turbo.sh\n    73→```\n    74→\n    75→## Dockerfile Management\n    76→\n    77→### Key Principles\n    78→1. **Append-only modifications**: Always add new dependencies at the end of Dockerfile to minimize rebuild time and leverage Docker layer caching\n    79→2. **HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1**: Hugging Face Hub is set to offline mode - all models must be pre-downloaded\n    80→3. **Skip model downloads**: `skip_download_model` flag prevents automatic downloads during plugin installation\n    81→\n    82→### Plugin Installation Pattern\n    83→Use ComfyUI Manager CLI (`cm-cli.py`) for plugin installation:\n    84→```dockerfile\n    85→RUN python3 $CUSTOM_MANAGER_CLI/cm-cli.py install <github_url>\n    86→```\n    87→\n    88→For specific commits:\n    89→```dockerfile\n    90→RUN python3 $CUSTOM_MANAGER_CLI/cm-cli.py install <github_url>@<commit_hash>\n    91→```\n    92→\n    93→### Environment Variables\n    94→- `$COMFYUI_PATH` - ComfyUI installation directory (typically `/workspace/ComfyUI`)\n    95→- `$CUSTOM_MANAGER_CLI` - ComfyUI Manager CLI path\n    96→\n    97→## Installed ComfyUI Plugins\n    98→\n    99→The Dockerfile maintains a comprehensive set of ComfyUI custom nodes including:\n   100→- Impact Pack, Inspire Pack, Essentials\n   101→- ControlNet Aux, Advanced ControlNet\n   102→- IP-Adapter Plus, IP-Adapter Flux\n   103→- Video Helper Suite, LivePortrait\n   104→- Segment Anything 2 & 3\n   105→- ReActor (face swap)\n   106→- SUPIR, GGUF support\n   107→- WanVideo Wrapper\n   108→- Various utility nodes (Crystools, rgthree-comfy, KJNodes, etc.)\n   109→\n   110→## Workflow Testing Process\n   111→\n   112→1. **Build image** with new plugins/dependencies\n   113→2. **Run container** locally with GPU access\n   114→3. **Start ComfyUI** using entrypoint script\n   115→4. **Test workflow** via port-forwarded web interface\n   116→5. **Download workflow** in API format from ComfyUI\n   117→6. **Push image** to platform using build script\n   118→7. **Upload workflow** to platform web console\n   119→8. **Test and deploy** in platform environment\n   120→\n   121→### Important Notes\n   122→- Workflows are lost on container restart when using `docker run` directly\n   123→- Using `entrypoint.sh` method preserves workflows between restarts (useful for debugging)\n   124→- Use `tmux` for persistent sessions that survive SSH disconnects\n   125→- Always download workflow files locally before container restart\n   126→\n   127→## Special Dependencies\n   128→\n   129→### Python Packages\n   130→Critical version pins:\n   131→```dockerfile\n   132→RUN pip install protobuf==4.25.6 timm==1.0.12 insightface\n   133→RUN TORCH_CUDA_ARCH_LIST=\"8.9\" pip install --no-build-isolation -U sageattention\n   134→```\n   135→\n   136→### Segment Anything 3\n   137→Requires post-install setup:\n   138→```dockerfile\n   139→RUN python3 $CUSTOM_MANAGER_CLI/cm-cli.py install \\\n   140→    https://github.com/ywythu/ComfyUI-segment-anything-3 \\\n   141→    && cd /workspace/ComfyUI/custom_nodes/ComfyUI-segment-anything-3/sam3/ \\\n   142→    && pip install -e . && pip install -e \".[notebooks, dev]\"\n   143→```\n   144→\n   145→## Platform Integration\n   146→\n   147→After building and testing locally:\n   148→1. New images must be deployed to platform before workflows can use them\n   149→2. Upload workflow API files through web console\n   150→3. Select appropriate environment for testing\n   151→4. Deploy to production after validation\n   152→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. It also brings meaningful upgrades to serving APIs, profiling & benchmarking, and overall stability. Please check our latest [paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02204) for architecture design and performance results.\n    21→- [2026/01] We released [0.12.0rc1](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-omni/releases/tag/v0.12.0rc1) - a major RC milestone focused on maturing the diffusion stack, strengthening OpenAI-compatible serving, expanding omni-model coverage, and improving stability across platforms (GPU/NPU/ROCm).\n    22→- [2025/11] vLLM community officially released [vllm-project/vllm-omni](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-omni) in order to support omni-modality models serving.\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## About\n    27→\n    28→[vLLM](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm) was originally designed to support large language models for text-based autoregressive generation tasks. vLLM-Omni is a framework that extends its support for omni-modality model inference and serving:\n    29→\n    30→- **Omni-modality**: Text, image, video, and audio data processing\n    31→- **Non-autoregressive Architectures**: extend the AR support of vLLM to Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and other parallel generation models\n    32→- **Heterogeneous outputs**: from traditional text generation to multimodal outputs\n    33→\n    34→<p align=\"center\">\n    35→  <picture>\n    36→    <img alt=\"vllm-omni\" src=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vllm-project/vllm-omni/refs/heads/main/docs/source/architecture/omni-modality-model-architecture.png\" width=55%>\n    37→  </picture>\n    38→</p>\n    39→\n    40→vLLM-Omni is fast with:\n    41→\n    42→- State-of-the-art AR support by leveraging efficient KV cache management from vLLM\n    43→- Pipelined stage execution overlapping for high throughput performance\n    44→- Fully disaggregation based on OmniConnector and dynamic resource allocation across stages\n    45→\n    46→vLLM-Omni is flexible and easy to use with:\n    47→\n    48→- Heterogeneous pipeline abstraction to manage complex model workflows\n    49→- Seamless integration with popular Hugging Face models\n    50→- Tensor, pipeline, data and expert parallelism support for distributed inference\n    51→- Streaming outputs\n    52→- OpenAI-compatible API server\n    53→\n    54→vLLM-Omni seamlessly supports most popular open-source models on HuggingFace, including:\n    55→\n    56→- Omni-modality models (e.g. Qwen-Omni)\n    57→- Multi-modality generation models (e.g. Qwen-Image)\n    58→\n    59→## Getting Started\n    60→\n    61→Visit our {"content": "# CLAUDE.md\n\nThis file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.\n\n## Project Overview\n\nThis is a multi-repo workspace for **vLLM-Omni** development - a framework for efficient omni-modality model inference and serving. It supports text, image, video, audio, and diffusion generation models.\n\n## Project Structure\n\n- `repos/vllm-omni/`: Primary Python library. Code under `vllm_omni/`, tests under `tests/`.\n- `repos/sglang/`: Local clone of SGLang (branch `feat/svdquant`) for diffusion experiments.\n- `workspace/experiments/`: Benchmark scripts.\n- `workspace/third_party/`: Third-party integrations (e.g., ComfyUI variants).\n- `workspace/docs/`: Notes, reports, and handoff documentation.\n- `data/models/`: Local model folders.\n- `data/weights/`: Shared weight store for `.safetensors` (do not commit).\n- `data/test_sets/`: Small image fixtures for repeatable runs.\n- `artifacts/`: Benchmark outputs and bundles.\n\n## Commands\n\n### vllm-omni Development\n\n```bash\n# Run all tests\ncd repos/vllm-omni && pytest -q\n\n# Run tests with specific markers\ncd repos/vllm-omni && pytest -q -m diffusion\ncd repos/vllm-omni && pytest -q -m omni\n\n# Install in development mode\ncd repos/vllm-omni && pip install -e .\n\n# Run pre-commit hooks\ncd repos/vllm-omni && pre-commit run --all-files\n```\n\n### Benchmarking\n\n```bash\n# Run diffusers+nunchaku baseline benchmark\nconda run -p .conda/envs/nunchaku-base \\\n  python workspace/experiments/bench_diffusers_qwen_edit.py \\\n  --quantized-transformer data/weights/nunchaku_qwen_image_edit_2511_ultimate_speed_int4.safetensors \\\n  --lora data/weights/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\n\n# SGLang diffusion CLI\nconda run -p .conda/envs/sglang-diff \\\n  sglang generate --model-path Qwen/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 --help\n```\n\n## Architecture\n\nThe vllm-omni module (`vllm_omni/`) is organized by functionality:\n\n- `entrypoints/`: CLI and API server entry points\n- `model_executor/`: Model loading and execution logic\n- `diffusion/`: Diffusion model support\n- `engine/`: Inference engine core\n- `distributed/`: Distributed execution support\n- `platforms/`: GPU/NPU/ROCm platform abstractions\n- `worker/`: Worker processes for inference\n\n## Code Style\n\n- Python: 4-space indentation, `snake_case` for functions/modules\n- Uses `pre-commit` with Ruff for linting/formatting\n- Ruff config: line-length 120, pyupgrade rules enabled\n- mypy strict mode enabled (Python 3.12/3.13)\n\n## Commit Conventions\n\nvllm-omni uses bracketed prefixes: `[Bugfix]`, `[Doc]`, `[Perf]`, etc.\nPRs should include what changed, how to reproduce, and performance numbers for diffusion/serving changes.\n\n## Agent Notes\n\n- Keep guidance in `AGENTS.md` rather than creating new rules files\n- Do not end responses with leading prompts\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/CLAUDE.md"}

根据我对代码库的分析，这是一个 **vLLM-Omni 开发工作空间**，主要用于多模态大模型的推理服务开发。

## 核心项目：vLLM-Omni

位于 `repos/vllm-omni/`，是一个开源框架，支持：
- **多模态**：文本、图像、视频、音频
- **非自回归架构**：Diffusion Transformers (DiT) 等并行生成模型
- **异构输出**：从传统文本生成到多模态生成

## 目录结构

| 目录 | 用途 |
|------|------|
| `repos/vllm-omni/` | 主代码库（Python 库 + CLI） |
| `repos/sglang/` | SGLang 本地克隆（用于 diffusion 实验） |
| `workspace/experiments/` | 基准测试脚本 |
| `workspace/third_party/` | 第三方集成（如 ComfyUI 变体） |
| `workspace/docs/` | 文档和笔记 |
| `data/models/` | 本地模型 |
| `data/weights/` | 共享权重存储（.safetensors） |

## 当前工作

从 `AGENTS.md` 和 benchmark 脚本来看，主要工作包括：

1. **diffusion 模型量化**：使用 nunchaku 进行图像编辑模型的量化加速
2. **文本编码器优化**：`experiments/bench_text_encoder.py` 进行文本编码器基准测试
3. **SGLang 集成**：测试 `sglang[diffusion]` 与量化模型的兼容性

## 技术栈

- PyTorch + CUDA
- vLLM-Omni (v0.14.0)
- SGLang
- diffusers
- 量化工具（nunchaku）

```
{"text": "1→# Offload (RAM↔VRAM) 测试记录与结论（slab/flat-packed + pinned）\n     2→\n     3→更新时间：2026-02-11\n     4→\n     5→本报告聚焦一个问题：**权重在 RAM↔VRAM 之间 swap（offload / reload）时，如何把带宽打满、并确保推理行为正确**。\n     6→\n     7→## TL;DR\n     8→\n     9→- **Pinned RAM 对 swap 带宽至关重要**：当 offload store 是 pinned（持久化 pinned buffer）时，RAM↔VRAM 的 D2H/H2D 能稳定到 **~18–24 GB/s**（接近 PCIe 上限）。pageable RAM 往往在 **~2–13 GB/s** 且波动大。\n    10→- **Flat/Packed (slab) 是“成熟工业方案”**：把几千个小 tensor 打包成少数几个大 slab（通常按 dtype），从而把“几千次 copy 调度”变成“几次大 copy”。\n    11→- **slab view 不会拖慢 forward**：在本仓库的全量 forward 测试中（TE + DiT），slab-backed 的 forward 时间与 baseline 几乎一致。\n    12→- **Nunchaku DiT forward 本身是非确定的**：即使同一个模型、同一输入、连续跑两次 forward，输出也会有可观的 max diff（~0.06–0.15 量级）。因此一致性验证应以“swap 后的误差不超过 intrinsic noise”为准，而不是强行 bitwise。\n    13→\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## 背景：为什么 pinned / slab 这么关键\n    17→\n    18→### 1) pinned RAM 为什么通常更快\n    19→\n    20→GPU 做 H2D / D2H 最理想路径是 DMA 直接读写 host memory；**DMA 需要 pinned（page-locked）内存**。\n    21→\n    22→当源是 pageable RAM 时，驱动通常会走隐式 staging：\n    23→\n    24→1. 临时找/申请一个 pinned staging buffer（或复用池）\n    25→2. CPU memcpy pageable → staging\n    26→3. DMA staging → VRAM\n    27→\n    28→这会引入额外 copy / 同步 / pin/unpin 开销，吞吐更低且更抖。\n    29→\n    30→### 2) “按 shard 合并/拼接再 H2D”是什么意思\n    31→\n    32→你的 checkpoint 往往是“成千上万个参数 tensor（散在一个或多个 shard）”。\n    33→\n    34→**flat/packed** 的做法是把这些小 tensor 离线打包成少数几个大块（slab），并记录 metadata（name→offset/shape/dtype）。\n    35→\n    36→加载时只需要：\n    37→\n    38→1. 分配少数几个 GPU slab\n    39→2. 做少数几次大 H2D\n    40→3. 用 view/slice 把参数指回 slab 的对应区域（零拷贝 view）\n    41→\n    42→收益点：\n    43→\n    44→- copy 调用：几千次 → 1~3 次\n    45→- allocator 调用：几千次 → 1~3 次\n    46→- 更容易 overlap（prefetch / double-buffer）\n    47→\n    48→### 3) “pinned 反而更慢”的常见误区\n    49→\n    50→如果你测的是“**把 pageable 权重 copy 到 pinned store** + 再 H2D”的总耗时，那 pinned 可能看起来更慢。\n    51→\n    52→成熟 offload 的前提是：\n    53→\n    54→- **offload store 本来就常驻 pinned**（只准备一次）\n    55→- swap 热路径只做 DMA copy\n    56→\n    57→本仓库新的 swap benchmark 已把“store_prepare_seconds（准备 pinned store）”也记录出来，避免误读。\n    58→\n    59→---\n    60→\n    61→## 实验环境\n    62→\n    63→- GPU：NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 (24GB)\n    64→- PyTorch：2.6.0+cu124\n    65→- Conda env：`.conda/envs/nunchaku-base`\n    66→\n    67→权重：\n    68→\n    69→- pinned 内存并 copy 数据（非常贵）\n   119→- pageable store_prepare 几乎为 0\n   120→\n   121→这解释了为什么“如果把准备 pinned store 的成本也算入 swap”，你会得到很奇怪的结论。\n   122→\n   123→**结论**：pinned 对“swap 热路径”很快，但 pinned store 必须持久化复用。\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## 2) 全量 forward：slab/packed 是否改变行为？是否拖慢推理？\n   128→\n   129→### 2.1 Text Encoder（Qwen2.5-VL）\n   130→\n   131→脚本：\n   132→\n   133→- baseline forward（2 iters）：0.862 s\n   155→- slab forward（2 iters）：0.865 s\n   156→\n   157→一致性解释：\n   158→\n   159→- 这里记录了：\n   160→  - baseline 重复 forward 的 max diff：0.0625\n   161→  - slab 重复 1.87s\n   180→\n   181→说明：即使把“分配+拷贝”总成本一起测，allocation 也不是主因；更大的问题来自：\n   182→\n   183→- pageable staging\n   184→- per-tensor 调度（成千次 small copy）\n   185→\n   186→因此更成熟的优化路线是：\n   187→\n   188→- slab 减少调度次数\n   189→- pinned 把 copy 吞吐打满\n   190→- 再考虑流水 prefetch/overlap\n   191→\n   192→---\n   193→\n   194→## 3.1) A/B/C 三种 H2D（RAM→VRAM）路径的严格对比（含 include-alloc 拆分）\n   195→\n   196→这里把 H2D 的实现按成熟度分成 3 类（对应 `experiments/archive/bench_offload_vram_to_ram.py` 的三条路径）：\n   197→\n   198→- A) **per-tensor**：对每个权重 tensor 独立分配 GPU tensor + H2D。\n   199→- B) **pack-per-shard**：对每个 shard 文件，把该 shard 内的 tensors 先按 dtype 拼接成少量大 buffer，再做 H2D。\n   200→- C) **streaming / double-buffer**：用固定大小的 staging buffer + 双缓冲，把“CPU pack”与“GPU H2D”流水化。\n   201→\n   202→数据（include-alloc 版，包含 `stage_seconds / alloc_seconds / h2d_seconds`）：\n   203→\n   204→- `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/results/h2d_matrix_r2/A_per_tensor_pinned_includealloc.json`\n   205→- 207→- `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/results/h2d_matrix_r2/B_pack_shard_unpinned_includealloc.json`\n   208→\n   209→### 关键观察：为什么有时 “pinned 反而更慢”？\n   210→\n   211→以 Text Encoder（16.58GB, 729 tensors）为例：\n   212→\n   213→- A/per-tensor + pinned：**端到端 2.19 GB/s**，但其中 `stage_seconds=5.318s`（`t_cpu.pin_memory()` 的 clone）占了绝大头；真正 DMA 的 `h2d_seconds=1.150s`，相当于 **~14.4 GB/s**。\n   214→- A/per-tensor + unpinned：**端到端 6.13 GB/s**，`stage_seconds≈0`，但 `h2d_seconds=2.661s`，相当于 **~6.2 GB/s**。\n   215→\n   216→这说明：\n   217→\n   218→- **pinned 的“DMA 拷贝”确实更快**（`h2d_seconds` 更短）。\n   219→- 但如果你在热路径里临时对每个 tensor 做 `pin_memory()`（pageable→pinned 的 memcpy），端到端会被 `stage_seconds` 吞掉。\n   220→\n   221→成熟 offload 的正确形态是：**CPU 侧 offload store 一开始就常驻 pinned（或 host_register pin）并长期复用**，swap 热路径只做 DMA。\n   222→\n   223→### 关键观察：allocation 不是主要瓶颈\n   224→\n   225→在 include-alloc 的结果里，`alloc_seconds` 普遍远小于 `stage_seconds` 和 `h2d_seconds`。\n   226→\n   227→例如（DiT, 11.52GB, 4093 tensors, A/per-tensor unpinned）：\n   228→\n   229→- `alloc_seconds=0.033s`\n   230→- `h2d_seconds=1.873s`\n   231→\n   232→因此“按 slab/packing 降低 copy 调度 + pinned 把 DMA 吞吐打满”才是更优先的优化方向。\n   233→\n   234→---\n   235→\n   236→## 4) torch.compile：能否 compile？建议如何处理？\n   237→\n   238→### Text Encoder\n   239→\n   240→TE（Transformers）一般 **可以 compile**，但首个 forward 会触发编译，成本很高。\n   241→\n   242→建议：\n   243→\n   244→- 把 compile 当作“常驻服务进程”的长期收益，而不是每次加载都 compile。\n   245→\n   246→补充：在本机（4090 / torch 2.6.0）上对 **Text Encoder 整体 `torch.compile`** 的实测表现是：\n   247→\n   248→- 在 120s 超时窗口内无法完成（很可能卡在编译/首次图捕获阶段），见：\n   249→  - `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/results/compile_bench/compile_bench_20260211_030542.json`\n   250→- 结论：如果要用 compile，建议只在“长驻进程”里做，并且要接受**首编译成本很大**；或者只 compile 子图/热点模块。\n   251→\n   252→### Nunchaku DiT\n   253→\n   254→在本环境下，`torch.compile` 对 Nunchaku 的 quant kernel/pos_embed 路径**容易失败**（Dynamo tracing 报错）。\n   255→\n   256→- 实测：`dit/compile` 直接报错（Dynamo tracing 时 `AttributeError: 'int' object has no attribute 'pos_freqs'`），见：\n   257→  - `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/results/compile_bench/compile_bench_20260211_030542.json`\n   258→\n   259→建议：\n   260→\n   261→- 不把 compile 作为 offload 方案的强依赖。\n   262→- 如果一定要 compile：\n   263→  - 只 compile 可编译的子模块（比如 CPU-side prepare、或 non-quant 部分）\n   264→\n   265→---\n   266→\n   267→## 5) ComfyUI 有没有做这些？\n   268→\n   269→ComfyUI 确实做了 pinned 管理，并且是“原地 pin”（`cudaHostRegister`）+预算控制：\n   270→\n   271→- `third_party/ComfyUI/comfy/model_management.py:1311`\n   272→\n   273→但它总体更偏“按 tensor 做 offload/pin”，不是“flat slab + offset metadata”这一套。\n   274→\n   275→---\n   276→\n   277→## 6) 下一步（建议）\n   278→\n   279→1. **把 offload store 设计成长期 pinned**（pinned pool / pinned slabs），swap 热路径只做 DMA copy。\n   280→2. **slab 按 layer/chunk 切分**（不是全模型一个 slab），实现：\n   281→   - 下一层预取（async H2D）\n   282→   - 上一层回收（async D2H）\n   283→   - compute/mem overlap\n   284→3. 如果采用 ComfyUI 风格的 `cudaHostRegister`：\n   285→   - 必须做 pinned 预算上限\n   286→   - 必须成对 unpin\n   287→   - 要考虑“同一 range 已经 mapped”的报错（多进程/复用场景会出现）\n   288→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. > AGENT
```

看到了！这是一个非常系统的 **RAM → VRAM 传输优化** 实验。让我给你总结一下：

## 实验背景

**目标**：在权重在 RAM ↔ VRAM 之间 swap（offload/reload）时，如何打满 PCIe 带宽并确保推理正确。

## 核心技术方案

### 1. Flat/Packed (Slab)
- **问题**：模型有几千个小的 tensor，直接逐个 H2D 会产生几千次 copy 调度
- **方案**：离线把几千个小 tensor 打包成少数几个大块（slab，按 dtype 分）
- **效果**：copy 调用从几千次 → 1~3 次

### 2. Pinned Memory (Page-locked)
- **问题**：pageable RAM 会走隐式 staging（CPU memcpy → pinned staging → DMA），吞吐低且抖动
- **方案**：使用 pinned memory 让 DMA 直接读写 host memory
- **效果**：带宽从 ~2-13 GB/s → 18-24 GB/s（接近 PCIe 上限）

## 关键结果

| 方法 | H2D 带宽 | D2H 带宽 |
|------|----------|----------|
| per-tensor pageable | ~10-13 GB/s | ~2-3 GB/s |
| per-tensor pinned | ~21 GB/s | ~18 GB/s |
| slab + pinned | ~21-22 GB/s | ~19-20 GB/s |

## 验证结论

- **slab view 不拖慢 forward**：TE 和 DiT 的 forward 时间与 baseline 几乎一致
- **swap 误差 ≤ intrinsic noise**：DiT 本身是非确定性的，swap 后的差异（~0.06-0.15）不超过模型本身的噪声
- **allocation 不是瓶颈**：alloc_seconds ~0.03s，h2d_seconds ~1.87s

## 下一步建议

1. offload store 设计成长期 pinned，swap 热路径只做 DMA
2. slab 按 layer/chunk 切分，实现 prefetch/overlap
3. 参考 ComfyUI 的 `cudaHostRegister` + 预算控制

---

## 清理计划

### 1. 完全删除（无用/过大）

| 路径 | 原因 |
|------|------|
| `repos/` | 外部仓库，可通过 git clone 重新获取 |
| `workspace/third_party/` | 第三方 ComfyUI 变体，不是核心工作 |
| `.cache/` | 缓存，可重新生成 |
| `.hf_cache/` | HuggingFace 缓存 |
| `runtime/` | conda 环境，可重建 |
| `.conda/` | conda 环境 |

### 2. 保留（核心工作）

| 路径 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| `data/weights/` | **核心权重** (nunchaku 量化模型、LoRA) |
| `data/models/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511/` | 原始模型 |
| `workspace/experiments/flat_slab/` | **核心 slab 实验脚本** |
| `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/` | **核心实验结果** |
| `workspace/docs/offload_slab_pinned_report.md` | **核心报告** |

### 3. 可选删除

| 路径 | 原因 |
|------|------|
| `workspace/experiments/archive/` | 旧实验，flat_slab 已经替代 |
| `data/test_sets/` | 测试数据，如果不需要 |
| `artifacts/archive/` | 旧输出 |
| `workspace/docs/root_legacy/` | 旧 ComfyUI 项目文档 |
| `workspace/docs/REORG_20260211.md` | 临时文档 |

### 4. 根目录文件

| 文件 | 操作 |
|------|------|
| `AGENTS.md` | 保留 |
| `CLAUDE.md` | 保留 |

---

```
/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/
├── AGENTS.md
├── CLAUDE.md

│   ├── models/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511/

│   ├── docs/

│   └── experiments/
│       └── flat_slab/

    └── ram_vram_transfer/
```

> AGENT

## artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/ 是什么

这是实验的**结果数据**目录，包含：
- `results/` - benchmark 输出的 JSON 数据（带宽、耗时、误差等）
- `bundles/` - 打包的实验配置和报告
- `reports/` - 报告文件

这些是验证 slab+pinned 方案有效性的**关键证据**。

---

## 简化目录结构

把 `flat_slab` 直接放到根目录，`artifacts` 也扁平化：

```
/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/
├── AGENTS.md
├── CLAUDE.md
├── data/
│   ├── models/Qwen-Image-Edit-2511/
│   └── weights/
├── flat_slab/                    # 实验脚本 (原 workspace/experiments/flat_slab/)
├── artifacts/                     # 实验结果
│   └── offload/                  # (原 ram_vram_transfer/)
└── docs/
    └── offload_slab_pinned_report.md
```

即：
- 删除 `workspace/` 整个目录
- `workspace/experiments/flat_slab/` → `flat_slab/`
- `artifacts/ram_vram_transfer/` → `artifacts/offload/`
- `workspace/docs/` → `docs/`

---

数据文件都在。但是由于之前的清理删除了 conda 环境，现在无法运行实验。让我给你总结这三个实验：

---

## 实验总结

### 实验 1: `bench_flat_packed_offload.py`

**目标**：测试 RAM↔VRAM swap 吞吐带宽

**核心对比**（5 种方案）：

| 方案 | 描述 |
|------|------|
| baseline per-tensor pageable | 逐个 tensor 复制，pageable RAM |
| baseline per-tensor pinned | 逐个 tensor 复制，pinned RAM (`pin_memory()`) |
| flat_packed slabs pageable | 打包成 slab（按 dtype），pageable |
| flat_packed slabs pinned | 打包成 slab + pinned_clone |
| flat_packed slabs host_register | 打包成 slab + `cudaHostRegister`（ComfyUI 风格）|

**测试对象**：
- DiT (Nunchaku int4): 11.52 GB, 4093 tensors → 3 slabs
- Text Encoder: 16.58 GB, 729 tensors → 1 slab

**输出**：`artifacts/offload/results/flat_packed_offload/*.json`

---

### 实验 2: `bench_full_forward_offload_flat_packed.py`

**目标**：验证 slab 方式是否改变模型 forward 结果 & 是否拖慢推理

**测试**：
1. **Text Encoder**：baseline vs slab-backed forward，对比 `prompt_embeds`
2. **DiT**：baseline vs slab-backed forward，对比输出
3. **Swap 影响**：做 D2H→H2D 后再次 forward，对比误差

**关键验证**：
- baseline vs slab forward 的 `max_abs_diff`
- baseline 重复 forward 的差异（控制组，因为 DiT 本身非确定性）
- swap 后差异 vs baseline 噪声

**输出**：`artifacts/offload/results/full_forward_offload/*.json`

---

### 实验 3: `validate_flat_packed_offload_inference.py`

**目标**：轻量级正确性验证（单元测试风格）

**方法**：
1. 从 slab 中抽取 12 个代表性的 tensor（不同 dtype/shape）
2. 对每个 tensor 做推理操作（matmul/conv2d/sum）
3. **swap 前后**分别运行 op，对比结果

**检查点**：
- `ok_values`: slab view 与原始 tensor 值是否相等
- `ok_op_before`: swap 前 op 结果是否一致
- `ok_op_after`: swap 后 op 结果是否一致

**输出**：`artifacts/offload/results/flat_packed_validate/*.json`

---

## 实验结论（根据代码和报告）

### 带宽结果

| 方法 | H2D | D2H |
|------|-----|-----|
| per-tensor pageable | ~10-13 GB/s | ~2-3 GB/s |
| per-tensor pinned | ~21 GB/s | ~18 GB/s |
| slab + pinned | ~21-22 GB/s | ~19-20 GB/s |

### 正确性

- **Text Encoder**：slab view 完全无差异（`max_abs_diff = 0`）
- **DiT**：swap 差异 ~0.06-0.15，不超过模型本身的非确定性噪声
- **推理时间**：slab 不拖慢 forward（~1.2s vs 1.15s）

---

/mnt/public/ 是各种公共模型和文件夹   /mnt/private/ 中是私有的
/mnt/private/models/是放我们自己的模型的，每次推送也会把最新的模型推上去
/mnt/private/workspace/是我们自己的文件夹，里面可以放脚本文件，
/mnt/private/workspace/Dockerfile 是我们每次需要维护的，每次最好是从后面添加，这样减少打包时间

## 工具选择讨论：mamba vs conda vs uv

| 工具 | 优点 | 缺点 |
|------|------|------|
| **conda** | 成熟稳定，生态大 | 慢，solve 经常卡 |
| **mamba** | C++ 重写，比 conda 快 10-20x，支持 conda-forge | 需要额外安装 |
| **uv** | 最快（Rust），现代化 | 主要是 pip 替代，conda 环境支持有限 |

### 建议

对于你的场景（PyTorch + CUDA + 大模型）：

1. **推荐 mamba** - 兼顾速度和兼容性
   ```bash
   mamba create -n nunchaku-base python=3.11 pytorch=2.6 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia
   ```

2. **如果追求极致速度** - 用 mamba 建基础环境 + uv 装 pip 包
   ```bash
   mamba create -n xxx python=3.11
   conda activate xxx
   uv pip install torch transformers ...
   ```

3. **conda** - 如果遇到奇怪的依赖冲突再用

```bash
# 1. 用 mamba 创建基础环境
mamba create -n slab python=3.11 -y

# 2. 激活环境
mamba activate slab

# 3. 用 mamba 安装 pytorch + cuda
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y

# 4. 用 uv 安装 pip 包（国内源）
uv pip install --index-url https://mirrors.aliyun.com/pypi/simple/ \
  transformers safetensors diffusers accelerate gradio soundfile \
  librosa resampy tqdm torchsde "imageio[ffmpeg]" onnxruntime sox \
  omegaconf pillow opencv-python numpy

# 5. 安装 nunchaku（如果需要）
uv pip install nunchaku-torch

# 6. 安装加速相关
uv pip install --index-url https://mirrors.aliyun.com/pypi/simple/ \
  fa3-fwd cache-dit
```

## 问题 1：每次 SSH 都显示欢迎信息

这是无问芯穹平台预置的，在 `/etc/issue`。

**恢复默认 Ubuntu 登录画面**：

```bash
# 方式 1：恢复默认
echo 'Ubuntu 22.04.4 LTS \n \l' | sudo tee /etc/issue

# 方式 2：清空（不显示任何信息）
sudo truncate -s 0 /etc/issue
```

---

## 问题 2：命令执行速度

我的命令通过 Claude Code CLI 执行，会有额外开销（网络 round-trip + CLI 解析）。你本地直接执行会更快。

> AGENT

我测试的几条命令执行都很快（date, pwd, echo）。

可能的原因：

1. **网络 round-trip** - 我的请求需要先发送到 Claude API 再执行，有额外延迟
2. **welcome banner** - 每次输出都显示平台欢迎 banner，增加感知时间
3. **某些命令** - 如 `mamba install` 这种需要 solve 的确实会慢

你可以自己运行这条命令：

```bash
echo 'Ubuntu 22.04.4 LTS \n \l' | tee /etc/issue
```

这个命令应该立刻执行完成，不会卡住。之前你执行失败可能是因为别的原因（比如权限需要 sudo）。

> AGENT

问题是 `sudo` 不保留 conda/mamba 的 PATH。你已经是 user_4813494d 了，不需要 sudo。

直接运行：

```bash
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

```bash
mamba clean -a
```

然后安装：

```bash
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

清华镜像源已失效。需要配置新的 conda 源（阿里云或中科大）：

```bash
# 配置 conda 源
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/pypi/simple/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/pytorch/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/conda-forge/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/pkgs/main/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/pkgs/r/
conda config --set show_channel_urls yes

# 或者用中科大源
# conda config --add channel https://mirrors.ustc.edu.cn/anaconda/pkgs/main/
# conda config --add channel https://mirrors.ustc.edu.cn/anaconda/cloud/pytorch/
# conda config --add channel https://mirrors.ustc.edu.cn/anaconda/cloud/conda-forge/
```

然后：

```bash
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

> AGENT

pypi 镜像不是 conda channel。正确的命令：

```bash
# 1. 配置 conda 源（阿里云）
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/pkgs/main/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/pkgs/r/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/pytorch/
conda config --add channel https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/conda-forge/
conda config --set show_channel_urls yes

# 2. 清理缓存
mamba clean -a

# 3. 安装 pytorch
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

> AGENT

问题找到了！你的 `.condarc` 使用清华镜像（已失效）。

手动编辑配置文件：

```bash
cat > ~/.condarc << 'EOF'
channels:
  - defaults
show_channel_urls: true
channel_alias: https://mirrors.aliyun.com
custom_channels:
  conda-forge: https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/conda-forge
  pytorch: https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/pytorch
  nvidia: https://mirrors.aliyun.com/anaconda/cloud/nvidia
EOF
```

然后：

```bash
mamba clean -a
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

阿里云镜像也有问题。改用默认源直接安装：

```bash
mamba install pytorch=2.6.0 pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

或者用 pip（更快）：

```bash
pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124
```

你想用哪个方式？如果用 pip，我来帮你执行。

> AGENT

安装的是 CPU 版本。需要安装 CUDA 版本：

```bash
mamba install pytorch torchvision torchaudio pytorch-cuda=12.4 -c pytorch -c nvidia -y
```

或者用 pip（更快更准）：

```bash
pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124
```

你想用哪个？

## 当前状态

- 安装了 PyTorch 2.5.1（而非 2.6）
- CUDA 12.4 ✓
- GPU RTX 4090 ✓

## 问题分析

conda 的 pytorch channel 可能没有 2.6 的 CUDA 构建版本。让我查一下正确的安装方式。

> AGENT

现在清楚了。

## 分析结论

| 方式 | 问题 |
|------|------|
| conda pytorch channel | 只有 CPU 版本 (cpu_generic) 或旧版 CUDA |
| conda-forge | 只有 CPU 版本 |

**最可靠的方式：pip 安装 PyTorch 官方 wheel**

## 完整环境安装方案

```bash
# 1. 创建纯 Python 环境
mamba create -n nunchaku-base python=3.11 -y
mamba activate nunchaku-base

# 2. 用 pip 安装 PyTorch (官方 wheel，最可靠)
pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124

# 3. 用 uv 安装其他依赖 (更快)
uv pip install transformers safetensors diffusers accelerate ...
```

要我按这个方案重新来一遍吗？

结果出来了。核心发现：

| 模型 | per_tensor_pinned | slab_pinned |
|------|-------------------|-------------|
| **DiT** (4093 tensors → 3 slabs) | H2D: 23.7, D2H: 22.5 | H2D: 21.8, D2H: 19.8 |
| **Text Encoder** (729 tensors → 1 slab) | H2D: 24.0, D2H: 22.7 | H2D: 24.2, D2H: 22.6 |

**结论**：使用 **pinned memory** 后，两种方式都已经接近 PCIe 带宽上限 (~24 GB/s)，差异不大。slab 的优势在 pinned 场景下不明显。

> AGENT

你说得对，让我解释一下实现：

## 实现细节

### per-tensor pinned
```python
# 1. 数据先加载到 GPU
gpu_tensors = [t.to(device) for _, t in tensors]

# 2. 创建 pinned CPU tensor (未初始化)
cpu_store = [torch.empty_like(t, device="cpu", pin_memory=True) for _, t in tensors]

# 3. D2H/H2D: 每个 tensor 单独 copy (4093 次 copy)
for t_gpu, t_cpu in zip(gpu_tensors, cpu_store):
    t_gpu.copy_(t_cpu)
```

### slab pinned
```python
# 1. 从磁盘加载 packed slab
cpu_slabs = _load_flat_slabs(packed_path)

# 2. 创建 pinned 副本
cpu_store = {k: v.pin_memory() for k, v in cpu_slabs.items()}

# 3. D2H/H2D: 按 slab 复制 (3 次 copy)
for k, cpu_v in cpu_store.items():
    gpu_slabs[k].copy_(cpu_v)
```

## 你的疑问

确实，理论上 per-tensor 有 4093 次 DMA 调用，slab 只有 3 次。但结果已经接近 PCIe 上限 (~24 GB/s)，说明：

1. **DMA 并行度高** - GPU 有多个 DMA 引擎
2. **带宽饱和** - 已经是 PCIe 带宽瓶颈，不是调度瓶颈

slab 的优势可能在**延迟/抖动**上体现，而不是带宽。

你觉得这个解释合理吗？或者你认为测试方式有问题？

```json
{"command": "mamba run -n nunchaku-base python << 'EOF'\nimport torch\nimport time\n\n# Test: single large copy vs many small copies with pinned memory\ndevice = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\ntorch.cuda.set_device(device)\n\ndef timed_h2d(src, dst, iterations=10):\n    # Warmup\n    for _ in range(3):\n        dst.copy_(src, non_blocking=True)\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    \n    # Measure\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(iterations):\n        start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        start.record()\n        dst.copy_(src, non_blocking=True)\n        end.record()\n        end.synchronize()\n        times.append(start.elapsed_time(end) / 1000.0)\n    return sum(times) / len(times)\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"VRAM <-> RAM 吞吐量深入测试 (Pinned Memory)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\n# Test 1: Single large tensor\nprint(\"\\n[测试1] 单个大 tensor (1GB)\")\nsize = 1024 * 1024 * 1024  # 1GB\nsrc_cpu = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\")\ndst_gpu = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\n# Pageable\nt = timed_h2d(src_cpu, dst_gpu)\nprint(f\"  Pageable H2D: {size/1e9/t:.1f} GB/s\")\n\n# Pinned\nsrc_pinned = src_cpu.pin_memory()\nt = timed_h2d(src_pinned, dst_gpu)\nprint(f\"  Pinned H2D:   {size/1e9/t:.1f} GB/s\")\n\n# Test 2: Many small tensors (simulate per-tensor)\nprint(\"\\n[测试2] 1000个小 tensor (每个 1MB)\")\nsmall_size = 1024 * 1024  # 1MB\nn_tensors = 1000\n\n# Pageable\nsrcs_page = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\") for _ in range(n_tensors)]\ndsts = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\") for _ in range(n_tensors)]\n\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor s, d in zip(srcs_page, dsts):\n    d.copy_(s, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nt_pageable = time.perf_counter() - start\ngb = (small_size * n_tensors) / 1e9\nprint(f\"  Pageable: {gb/t_pageable:.1f} GB/s\")\n\n# Pinned\nsrcs_pin = [s.pin_memory() for s in srcs_page]\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor s, d in zip(srcs_pin, dsts):\n    d.copy_(s, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nt_pinned = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"  Pinned:   {gb/t_pinned:.1f} GB/s\")\n\n# Test 3: Slab (pack all into one large buffer)\nprint(\"\\n[测试3] Slab 方式 (打包成 1GB)\")\nslab_cpu = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\")\nslab_pinned = slab_cpu.pin_memory()\nslab_gpu = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\nt = timed_h2d(slab_pinned, slab_gpu)\nprint(f\"  Slab pinned H2D: {size/1e9/t:.1f} GB/s\")\n\n# Test 4: Async stream test\nprint(\"\\n[测试4] 多 stream 并行\")\nstreams = [torch.cuda.Stream() for _ in range(4)]\nsrc_multi = [torch.empty(size // 4 // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory() for _ in range(4)]\ndst_multi = [torch.empty(size // 4 // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\") for _ in range(4)]\n\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor i in range(4):\n    with torch.cuda.stream(streams[i]):\n        dst_multi[i].copy_(src_multi[i], non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nt = time.perf_counter() - start\ngb = (size / 1e9)\nprint(f\"  4 streams parallel: {gb/t:.1f} GB/s\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nEOF\n"}
```

```json
{"command": "mamba run -n nunchaku-base python << 'EOF'\nimport torch\nimport time\n\ndevice = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\ntorch.cuda.set_device(device)\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"开销分析: allocation vs copy\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nsize = 1024 * 1024 * 1024  # 1GB\n\n# Test 1: Allocation overhead\nprint(\"\\n[1] GPU 分配开销\")\niters = 10\n\n# Fresh allocation each time\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor _ in range(iters):\n    dst = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nalloc_time = (time.perf_counter() - start) / iters\nprint(f\"  新分配 1GB: {alloc_time*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\n# Reuse allocation\ndst = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor _ in range(iters):\n    dst.fill_(0)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nreuse_time = (time.perf_counter() - start) / iters\nprint(f\"  复用分配: {reuse_time*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\n# Test 2: Single large copy\nprint(\"\\n[2] 单次大 copy\")\nsrc_pinned = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory()\ndst = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\n# Measure copy only\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst.copy_(src_pinned, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\ncopy_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"  Copy 1GB: {copy_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/copy_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n# Test 3: Many small copies\nprint(\"\\n[3] 多次小 copy\")\nn = 1000\nsmall_size = size // n\nsrcs = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory() for _ in range(n)]\ndsts = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\") for _ in range(n)]\n\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor s, d in zip(srcs, dsts):\n    d.copy_(s, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\ntotal_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"  1000 x 1MB: {total_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/total_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\nprint(f\"  单次开销: {(total_time - copy_time)*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\n# Test 4: Same large buffer, many views\nprint(\"\\n[4] 同一 buffer 的多个 view\")\nsrc_big = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory()\ndst_big = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\nchunk_size = size // n\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor i in range(n):\n    src_view = src_big[i*chunk_size//4:(i+1)*chunk_size//4]\n    dst_view = dst_big[i*chunk_size//4:(i+1)*chunk_size//4]\n    dst_view.copy_(src_view, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nview_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"  1000 views: {view_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/view_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nEOF\n"}
```

> AGENT

## 最终结论

使用**实际 DiT 模型权重**（4093 个 tensor）测试：

| 方法 | H2D | D2H |
|------|-----|-----|
| **per-tensor pinned** | 23.5 GB/s | 22.9 GB/s |
| **slab packed** | 23.8 GB/s | 22.4 GB/s |

**结论**：

1. **pinned memory 是关键**：一旦使用 pinned memory，两种方式都接近 PCIe 上限 (~24 GB/s)
2. **slab 优势不明显**：在这个测试中，slab 并没有带来明显提升
3. **DMA 高效并行**：即使 4093 次小 copy，DMA 引擎也能流水线化

**真正有价值的优化方向**：
- 确保 offload store 持久化使用 pinned memory
- 预取/双缓冲流水化（prefetch/overlap compute and mem）
- 按 layer chunk 分片 slab，实现层间 overlap

> AGENT

找到了！ComfyUI 的实现机制：

## ComfyUI Pinned Memory 机制

### 核心实现 (`model_management.py`)
```python
# 1. 预算控制 - 限制 pinned 内存上限
MAX_PINNED_MEMORY = get_total_memory(cpu) * 0.95  # Linux

# 2. 使用 cudaHostRegister 原地 pin (不是 clone)
torch.cuda.cudart().cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, 1)

# 3. 追踪已 pinned 的内存总量
TOTAL_PINNED_MEMORY += size
if TOTAL_PINNED_MEMORY > MAX_PINNED_MEMORY:
    return False  # 超过预算，不再 pin
```

### 模型 offload 时 (`model_patcher.py`)
```python
# 卸载模型时 pin 权重到 CPU
def pin_weight_to_device(self, key):
    if comfy.model_management.pin_memory(weight):  # 尝试 pin
        self.pinned.add(key)

# 加载模型时 unpin
def unpin_weight(self, key):
    comfy.model_management.unpin_memory(weight)
```

### 关键特点

| 特性 | ComfyUI |
|------|---------|
| Pin 方式 | `cudaHostRegister` 原地 pin |
| 预算 | RAM 的 95%（Linux）|
| 粒度 | 按 module/weight 级别 |
| 优势 | 不用 clone，节省内存 |

---

## 我们的方案对比

| | ComfyUI | 我们的方案 |
|--|---------|-----------|
| Pin 方式 | `cudaHostRegister` | `pin_memory()` (clone) |
| 粒度 | per-weight | per-slab |
| 预算控制 | 有 | 无 |

> AGENT

## ComfyUI 启用 Pinned Memory

### 默认开启
Pinned memory **默认启用**，无需额外配置。

### 禁用方式
```bash
# 启动时添加参数禁用 pinned memory
python main.py --disable-pinned-memory
```

### 查看状态
启动时日志会显示：
```
Enabled pinned memory XXXXXX  # MB 为单位
```

### 预算配置
在 `comfy/model_management.py` 中：
```python
# Linux: 使用 95% 的 CPU 内存
MAX_PINNED_MEMORY = get_total_memory(torch.device("cpu")) * 0.95

# Windows: 使用 45%
MAX_PINNED_MEMORY = get_total_memory(torch.device("cpu")) * 0.45
```

## ComfyUI Offload 机制总结

### 启动参数

```bash
# VRAM 模式
--gpu-only        # 所有模型都在 GPU
--highvram        # 模型用完后留在 GPU
--normalvram     # 正常模式（用完卸载到 CPU）
--lowvram         # 低显存模式，分片加载 UNet
--novram          # 极低显存

# Async offload (默认开启)
--async-offload           # 启用异步 offload (默认 2 streams)
--async-offload 4         # 自定义 stream 数量
--disable-async-offload   # 禁用异步 offload

# Pinned memory
# 默认启用，无需参数
--disable-pinned-memory   # 禁用 pinned memory
```

### Offload 策略

| 组件 | 默认行为 |
|------|----------|
| **UNet** | 用完卸载到 CPU (normalvram) |
| **Text Encoder** | 用完卸载到 CPU |
| **VAE** | 用完卸载到 CPU |

这确实是**互斥**的 - 同一时间只有一个模型在 VRAM 中！

### 速度慢的原因

1. **默认是 sync copy** - 没有用 pinned memory 的异步特性
2. **per-tensor copy** - 每个权重单独传输 (可能有 1000+ 次)
3. **CPU 分配开销** - 每次传输前需要分配 GPU buffer

### 建议优化

如果 ComfyUI 慢，可以尝试：
```bash
# 启用 async offload
python main.py --async-offload 4

# 或者用 highvram 模式
python main.py --highvram
```

你想让我测试一下 ComfyUI 的实际传输速度吗？

> DEVELOPER

等一下 你的意思是 哦 你的意思是 他还要经历vram->ram!! 如果我们把两份权重都放 ram 中 只卸载 不回传 对吧 第二个问题 sync copy 和 per-tensor copy是什么 差异在哪 这非常有趣 请你仔细思考 请你仔细看看你

> AGENT

你说得非常对！让我仔细分析：

## 关键洞察

### ComfyUI 当前流程
```
Model in VRAM → 推理完成 → D2H (卸载到 CPU RAM)
                ↓
需要使用时 → H2D (从 CPU 加载回 VRAM)
```

每次 offload 都做了 **D2H + H2D**，两次传输！

### 你的提议
```
权重永久留在 pinned RAM
推理时 → H2D (pinned → VRAM)
推理后 → 直接释放 VRAM (不用 D2H 回传！)
```

**只做 H2D，不做 D2H！** 这确实更高效。

---

## Sync vs Async, Per-tensor vs Bulk

让我用代码验证：

Sync vs Async Copy\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nsize = 1024 * 1024 * 1024  # 1GB\nsrc = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory()\ndst = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\n# Sync copy\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst.copy_(src)  # sync\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nsync_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"\\nSync copy (1GB): {sync_time*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\n# Async copy  \ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst.copy_(src, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nasync_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"Async copy (1GB): {async_time*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\nprint(\"\\n结论: PyTorch 的 copy_ 默认已经很快了，主要瓶颈在 PCIe\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"2. Per-tensor vs Bulk (Pinned Memory)\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\n# Case A: 1000 small tensors (per-tensor)\nn = 1000\nsmall_size = size // n\nsrcs = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory() for _ in range(n)]\ndsts = [torch.empty(small_size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\") for _ in range(n)]\n\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\nfor s, d in zip(srcs, dsts):\n    d.copy_(s, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nper_tensor_time = time.perf_counter() - start\n\ntotal = sum(d.numel() * d.element_size() for d in dsts)\nprint(f\"\\nPer-tensor (1000 x 1MB): {per_tensor_time*1000:.1f} ms ({total/1e9/per_tensor_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n# Case B: Single bulk copy\nsrc_bulk = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\").pin_memory()\ndst_bulk = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst_bulk.copy_(src_bulk, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nbulk_time = time.perf_counter() - start\n\nprint(f\"Bulk (1GB): {bulk_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/bulk_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n差异: {(per_tensor_time - bulk_time)*1000:.1f} ms\")\nprint(\"结论: Pinned memory 下，per-tensor 开销极小！\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"3. 真正的瓶颈: Pageable vs Pinned\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\n# Pageable\nsrc_page = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cpu\")\ndst_page = torch.empty(size // 4, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst_page.copy_(src_page, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\npage_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"\\nPageable: {page_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/page_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n# Pinned\nsrc_pin = src_page.pin_memory()\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\nstart = time.perf_counter()\ndst_page.copy_(src_pin, non_blocking=True)\ntorch.cuda.synchronize()\npin_time = time.perf_counter() - start\nprint(f\"Pinned: {pin_time*1000:.1f} ms ({size/1e9/pin_time:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n提升: {page_time/pin_time:.1f}x\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nEOF\n"}

## 关键发现！

| 测试 | 结果 |
|------|------|
| Sync vs Async | **几乎无差异** - PyTorch copy 默认就很快 |
| Per-tensor vs Bulk (pinned) | **per-tensor 反而略快** - DMA 并行化 |
| **Pageable vs Pinned** | **2.9x 提升** - 关键瓶颈！ |

---

## 你说对了！

### ComfyUI 当前流程（有问题的）
```
1. 推理完成 → D2H (pageable，默认！) → 慢！
2. 下次需要 → H2D (pageable，默认！) → 又慢！
```

### 正确的优化
```
权重永久留在 pinned RAM：
1. 需要推理 → H2D (pinned) → 快！
2. 推理完成 → 直接释放 VRAM (del tensor) → 不用 D2H 回传！
```

**核心洞察**：根本不需要 D2H 回传！直接 `del` 释放 VRAM 即可，下次需要时直接从 pinned RAM H2D。

---

> AGENT

看到了！ComfyUI 的 `cast_to` 使用 `non_blocking=True`，但关键问题是：

## ComfyUI 的 offload 流程

```
1. 尝试 pin CPU 端的 weight (pinned_memory.py)
2. 使用 non_blocking copy 到/从 pinned memory
```

**但问题**：
- 如果 pinned memory 预算用完，会 fallback 到 pageable
- 每次 offload 都做 D2H + H2D（两次传输）

---

## 你提议的优化

```
优化前 (ComfyUI):
  推理 → D2H (pageable/pinned) → 需要 → H2D (pageable/pinned)

优化后:
  推理 → 直接 del VRAM (不 D2H!) → 需要 → H2D (pinned)
```

**只做 H2D，不回传！** 这确实是更高效的思路。

---

## 实验：模拟你的优化方案

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
```json
{"command": "mamba run -n nunchaku-base python << 'EOF'\nimport torch\nimport time\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ndevice = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\ntorch.cuda.set_device(device)\n\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"模拟优化方案: 只 H2D，不 D2H 回传\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\n# Load DiT weights to pinned memory (simulating persistent store)\ndit_file = \"data/weights/nunchaku_qwen_image_edit_2511_ultimate_speed_int4.safetensors\"\n\nprint(\"\\n[1] 加载权重到 pinned CPU memory\")\nweights = []\nwith safe_open(dit_file, framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n    for key in f.keys():\n        weights.append(f.get_tensor(key))\n\n# Pin all weights\npinned_weights = [w.pin_memory() for w in weights]\ntotal_bytes = sum(t.numel() * t.element_size() for t in pinned_weights)\nprint(f\"  Loaded: {len(pinned_weights)} tensors, {total_bytes/1e9:.1f} GB pinned\")\n\nprint(\"\\n[2] 模拟推理循环: H2D → compute → del VRAM (不D2H!)\")\n\ndef workflow_pinned_no_d2h(pinned_weights, device, iters=5):\n    \"\"\"优化后的流程: 只 H2D，不回传\"\"\"\n    times_h2d = []\n    \n    for i in range(iters):\n        # Allocate GPU buffer\n        gpu_weights = [torch.empty_like(w, device=device) for w in pinned_weights]\n        \n        # H2D: pinned -> VRAM\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        for gpu_w, pin_w in zip(gpu_weights, pinned_weights):\n            gpu_w.copy_(pin_w, non_blocking=True)\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        h2d_time = time.perf_counter() - start\n        times_h2d.append(h2d_time)\n        \n        # 模拟 compute\n        # ...\n        \n        # 释放 VRAM - 不回传！\n        del gpu_weights\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    \n    return times_h2d\n\ndef workflow_with_d2h(pinned_weights, device, iters=5):\n    \"\"\"原始流程: H2D → compute → D2H\"\"\"\n    times_h2d = []\n    times_d2h = []\n    \n    for i in range(iters):\n        gpu_weights = [torch.empty_like(w, device=device) for w in pinned_weights]\n        \n        # H2D\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        for gpu_w, pin_w in zip(gpu_weights, pinned_weights):\n            gpu_w.copy_(pin_w, non_blocking=True)\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        h2d_time = time.perf_counter() - start\n        times_h2d.append(h2d_time)\n        \n        # compute\n        # ...\n        \n        # D2H - 这里的开销！\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        for pin_w, gpu_w in zip(pinned_weights, gpu_weights):\n            pin_w.copy_(gpu_w, non_blocking=True)\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        d2h_time = time.perf_counter() - start\n        times_d2h.append(d2h_time)\n        \n        del gpu_weights\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    \n    return times_h2d, times_d2h\n\n# Run optimized (no D2H)\nprint(\"\\n[方案A] 只 H2D，不 D2H (优化)\")\ntimes_a = workflow_pinned_no_d2h(pinned_weights, device)\navg_a = sum(times_a) / len(times_a)\nprint(f\"  平均 H2D: {avg_a*1000:.1f} ms ({total_bytes/1e9/avg_a:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n# Run original (with D2H)\nprint(\"\\n[方案B] H2D + D2H (原始)\")\ntimes_b_h2d, times_b_d2h = workflow_with_d2h(pinned_weights, device)\navg_b_h2d = sum(times_b_h2d) / len(times_b_h2d)\navg_b_d2h = sum(times_b_d2h) / len(times_b_d2h)\nprint(f\"  平均 H2D: {avg_b_h2d*1000:.1f} ms ({total_bytes/1e9/avg_b_h2d:.1f} GB/s)\")\nprint(f\"  平均 D2H: {avg_b_d2h*1000:.1f} ms ({total_bytes/1e9/avg_b_d2h:.1f} GB/s)\")\nprint(f\"  总计:     {(avg_b_h2d + avg_b_d2h)*1000:.1f} ms\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(f\"优化效果: 每次 swap 节省 {avg_b_d2h*1000:.1f} ms\")\nprint(f\"带宽提升: H2D-only vs H2D+D2H = {total_bytes/1e9/avg_a:.1f} vs {total_bytes/1e9/(avg_b_h2d+avg_b_d2h):.1f} GB/s\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\nEOF\n"}
```

## 重大发现！

| 方案 | 每次 Swap 耗时 | 有效带宽 |
|------|----------------|----------|
| **H2D only (优化)** | 552 ms | **21 GB/s** |
| H2D + D2H (原始) | 1170 ms | 9.8 GB/s |

**提升：2.1x 速度！**

## 结论

你的优化思路完全正确：

```
权重永久留在 pinned CPU RAM：
1. 需要推理 → H2D (从 pinned)
2. 推理完成 → 直接 del VRAM (不用 D2H 回传！)
3. 下次需要 → 再次 H2D
```

**省掉了 D2H 这步！**

这就是 ComfyUI 慢的原因 - 它每次都做 D2H + H2D，而我们只需要 H2D。

```json
{"content": "# RAM <-> VRAM Offload 实验报告\n\n## 背景\n\n研究目标是优化 AI 模型推理时，权重在 RAM ↔ VRAM 之间的传输效率。\n\n## 实验环境\n\n- GPU: NVIDIA RTX 4090\n- PyTorch: 2.6.0 + cu124\n- 测试模型: Qwen2.5-VL-7B (Text Encoder) + DiT (Nunchaku int4)\n\n---\n\n## 核心发现\n\n### 1. Pinned Memory 是关键\n\n| 方式 | H2D 带宽 | D2H 带宽 |\n|------|----------|----------|\n| Pageable | ~5 GB/s | ~3 GB/s |\n| **Pinned** | **~21 GB/s** | **~19 GB/s** |\n\n**提升: 3-4x**\n\n### 2. Per-tensor vs Bulk (使用 Pinned 时)\n\n使用 pinned memory 后，per-tensor copy 和 bulk copy 几乎无差异：\n- 单次 1GB copy: 50ms (21 GB/s)\n- 1000 次 1MB copy: 51ms (21 GB/s)\n\nDMA 引擎可以高效流水线化多个小传输。\n\n### 3. 优化: 只 H2D，不 D2H 回传\n\n**关键洞察**: 权重可以永久留在 pinned CPU RAM，不需要每次都 D2H 回传。\n\n| 方案 | 每次 Swap | 有效带宽 |\n|------|-----------|----------|\n| H2D + D2H (原始) | 1170 ms | 9.8 GB/s |\n| **H2D only (优化)** | **552 ms** | **21 GB/s** |\n\n**提升: 2.1x**\n\n---\n\n## 优化方案\n\n### 原理\n\n```\n原始流程 (ComfyUI):\n  推理完成 → D2H (回传到 CPU) → 需要 → H2D (加载回 VRAM)\n\n优化流程:\n  权重永久留在 pinned RAM\n  需要推理 → H2D (从 pinned)\n → 直接 del  推理完成 VRAM (不 D2H 回传!)\n```\n\n### 实现要点\n\n1. **使用 pinned memory**: 用 `cudaHostRegister` 或 `pin_memory()` 保持权重在 pinned RAM\n2. **只 H2D**: 推理完成后直接释放 VRAM，不回传\n3. **预算控制**: 限制 pinned memory 上限 (如 RAM 的 95%)\n\n---\n\n## ComfyUI 分析\n\n### 启动参数\n\n```bash\n# VRAM 模式\n--gpu-only        # 所有模型都在 GPU\n--highvram        # 模型用完后留在 GPU\n--normalvram     # 正常模式（用完卸载到 CPU）\n--lowvram         # 低显存模式\n\n# Async offload (默认 2 streams)\n--async-offload 4\n--disable-async-offload\n\n# Pinned memory (默认启用)\n--disable-pinned-memory\n```\n\n### 当前问题\n\n1. 默认使用 pageable memory (除非 pinned 成功)\n2. 每次 offload 做 D2H + H2D (两次传输)\n3. pinned memory 预算有限，可能 fallback 到 pageable\n\n---\n\n## 实验脚本\n\n位于 `flat_slab/`:\n\n- `bench_flat_packed_offload.py` - 带宽基准测试\n\n---\n\n## 下一步\n\n1. 实现优化的 offload store (pinned only, no D2H)\n2. 按 layer/chunk 分片 slab，实现 prefetch/overlap\n3. 与 ComfyUI 集成测试\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/docs/OFFLOAD_EXPERIMENT.md"}
```

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:

   - Download model weights to correct ComfyUI directories

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - RAM↔VRAM offload optimization (flatten/slab/pinned memory)
   - Pinned memory (cudaHostRegister) vs pageable memory - 2.9x improvement
   - Per-tensor copy vs bulk copy - minimal difference with pinned memory
   - Key insight: Keep weights in pinned RAM, only H2D when needed, no D2H needed after inference
   - ComfyUI VRAM modes: --gpu-only, --highvram, --normalvram, --lowvram, --novram
   - ComfyUI async offload: --async-offload (default 2 streams)
   - Pinned memory: default enabled, --disable-pinned-memory to disable
   - H2D-only optimization achieves 2.1x speed vs H2D+D2H

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/CLAUDE.md` - Created for future Claude Code instances
   - `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/docs/OFFLOAD_EXPERIMENT.md` - Documents experiment findings:
     - Pinned memory gives 3-4x bandwidth improvement
     - Per-tensor vs bulk makes minimal difference with pinned
     - H2D-only optimization achieves 2.1x speed
   - `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/bench_flat_packed_offload.py` - Modified to compare only per_tensor_pinned vs slab_pinned
   - `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/scripts/download_weights.sh` - Created for downloading model weights
   - `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui/` - ComfyUI code and models directory

> AGENT

根据日志分析，ComfyUI的流程是：

**日志解读：**
1. `Requested to load QwenImageTEModel_` - 请求加载TE（文本编码器）
2. `Unloaded partially: 5266.71 MB freed` - 卸载之前模型的一部分权重（从VRAM移到CPU，这是D2H）
3. `loaded completely; 8934.81 MB usable` - TE加载完成（从CPU移到VRAM，这是H2D）
4. 然后加载VAE，同样的流程
5. `Prompt executed in 11.83 seconds` - 整个prompt耗时

**关键发现：**
- ComfyUI用的是`.to(device)`做内存传输，内部调用PyTorch的copy_
- 默认是**pageable memory**，没有使用pinned memory优化
- 每次load/unload都是**同步**传输（D2H+H2D两次）

---

**测量方案：**

写一个简单的benchmark脚本，hook PyTorch的tensor.to()或者用CUDA events来测量：

```python
# 方案1: Hook torch.Tensor.to()
original_to = torch.Tensor.to
def timed_to(self, *args, **kwargs):
    if args and isinstance(args[0], torch.device):
        direction = "H2D" if args[0].type == "cuda" else "D2H"
        start = time.time()
        result = original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)
        torch.cuda.synchronize()  # 确保传输完成
        print(f"{direction}: {(time.time()-start)*1000:.1f}ms, {self.nelement()*self.element_size()/1e9/(time.time()-start):.1f} GB/s")
        return result
    return original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)
torch.Tensor.to = timed_to
```

或者用CUDA events更精确：
```python
start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
start.record()
tensor = tensor.to(device)
end.record()
torch.cuda.synchronize()
print(f"H2D: {start.elapsed_time(end):.1f}ms")
```

> AGENT

确认了！让我给你总结：

## ComfyUI pinned memory 机制

**默认: 启用 pinned memory** (`model_management.py:1275`)
```python
if not args.disable_pinned_memory:
    if is_nvidia() or is_amd():
        MAX_PINNED_MEMORY = get_total_memory(cpu) * 0.95  # Linux: 95% RAM
```

**预算限制:**
- Linux: RAM的95%
- Windows: RAM的45%

**关键代码路径 (`ops.py:102-138`):**
```python
pin = comfy.pinned_memory.get_pin(s)  # 检查是否已pin过
if pin is not None:
    xfer_source = [pin]  # 从pinned memory传输
else:
    pin = comfy.pinned_memory.pin_memory(s)  # 尝试pin
    if pin is not None:
        xfer_source = [pin]
    else:
        # fallback到pageable memory!
        xfer_source = [s.weight, s.bias]
```

**结论：**
1. **默认尝试用pinned memory**
2. **如果预算超限，会fallback到pageable memory**
3. 你的日志显示加载了 ~8GB TE + ~19GB DiT，总共 ~27GB
4. 如果系统RAM不够95%，会部分使用pageable

**要确认你的情况：**
看ComfyUI启动时的日志，有没有打印 `Enabled pinned memory XXXX MB`？如果没有，说明可能pinned memory初始化失败了。

要测量实际是pinned还是pageable，可以写个脚本hook `cudaHostRegister` 看是否被调用。

> TOOL

tool_use Write

```json
{"content": "\"\"\"\nProfiling hook for measuring H2D/D2H bandwidth in ComfyUI\nUsage: Import before running ComfyUI\n    import sys\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab')\n    from profiler_hook import install_hooks\n    install_hooks()\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nimport time\nimport logging\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"ProfilingHook\")\n\n# Stats storage\nstats = defaultdict(lambda: {\"count\": 0, \"total_bytes\": 0, \"total_time\": 0})\n\n# Backup originals\n_original_to = torch.Tensor.to\n_original_cuda = torch.Tensor.cuda\n_original_cpu = torch.Tensor.cpu\n\ndef _measure_transfer(direction, self, *args, **kwargs):\n    \"\"\"Hook for tensor.to(), cuda(), cpu() methods\"\"\"\n    # Determine direction\n    if direction == \"to\":\n        if not args:\n            return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n        device = args[0]\n        if isinstance(device, torch.device):\n            if device.type == \"cuda\":\n                direction = \"H2D\"\n            elif device.type == \"cpu\" and self.device.type == \"cuda\":\n                direction = \"D2H\"\n            else:\n                return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n        else:\n            return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n    elif direction == \"cuda\":\n        direction = \"H2D\"\n    elif direction == \"cpu\":\n        direction = \"D2H\"\n    else:\n        return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n\n    # Skip if already on target device\n    if direction == \"H2D\" and self.is_cuda:\n        return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n    if direction == \"D2H\" and not self.is_cuda:\n        return _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n\n    # Measure\n    bytes_size = self.nelement() * self.element_size()\n\n    # Use CUDA events for accurate timing\n    start_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n    end_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n\n    start_event.record()\n    result = _original_to(self, *args, **kwargs)\n    end_event.record()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n\n    elapsed_ms = start_event.elapsed_time(end_event)\n    bandwidth_gb = bytes_size / (elapsed_ms * 1e-3) / 1e9 if elapsed_ms > 0 else 0\n\n    # Record stats\n    stats[direction][\"count\"] += 1\n    stats[direction][\"total_bytes\"] += bytes_size\n    stats[direction][\"total_time\"] += elapsed_ms\n\n    # Log large transfers (>10MB or slow)\n    if bytes_size > 10 * 1024 * 1024 or elapsed_ms > 50:\n        logger.info(f\"[{direction}] {bytes_size / 1e6:.1f} MB in {elapsed_ms:.1f}ms ({bandwidth_gb:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n    return result\n\ndef _hooked_to(self, *args, **kwargs):\n    return _measure_transfer(\"to\", self, *args, **kwargs)\n\ndef _hooked_cuda(self, *args, **kwargs):\n    return _measure_transfer(\"cuda\", self, *args, **kwargs)\n\ndef _hooked_cpu(self, *args, **kwargs):\n    return _measure_transfer(\"cpu\", self, *args, **kwargs)\n\ndef install_hooks():\n    \"\"\"Install profiling hooks\"\"\"\n    torch.Tensor.to = _hooked_to\n    torch.Tensor.cuda = _hooked_cuda\n    torch.Tensor.cpu = _hooked_cpu\n    logger.info(\"Profiling hooks installed for H2D/D2H\")\n\ndef uninstall_hooks():\n    \"\"\"Restore original methods\"\"\"\n    torch.Tensor.to = _original_to\n    torch.Tensor.cuda = _original_cuda\n    torch.Tensor.cpu = _original_cpu\n\ndef print_summary():\n    \"\"\"Print profiling summary\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 50)\n    logger.info(\"H2D/D2H Transfer Summary\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 50)\n\n    for direction in [\"H2D\", \"D2H\"]:\n        s = stats[direction]\n        if s[\"count\"] > 0:\n            avg_time = s[\"total_time\"] / s[\"count\"]\n            total_gb = s[\"total_bytes\"] / 1e9\n            avg_bw = total_gb / (s[\"total_time\"] * 1e-3) if s[\"total_time\"] > 0 else 0\n            logger.info(f\"{direction}: {s['count']} transfers, {total_gb:.2f} GB total, {avg_time:.1f}ms avg, {avg_bw:.1f} GB/s avg\")\n\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 50)\n\n# Auto-install if imported\ninstall_hooks()\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/profiler_hook.py"}
```

启动方式：
```bash
cd /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui
./run_with_profiler.sh
```

或者带参数：
```bash
cd /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui
./run_with_profiler.sh --listen 0.0.0.0 --port 8188
```

运行后：
- 每次 >10MB 的传输会打印带宽
- ComfyUI关闭时打印总览统计

如果要测特定workflow，可以在Web界面操作完后 `Ctrl+C` 终止，会打印summary。

> AGENT

这个结果很正常 - 说明hook没有正确捕获到ComfyUI的实际传输！

**问题：**
- ComfyUI不用`.to()`，而是用`.copy_()`做传输
- Hook需要监测`torch.copy_()`而不是`.to()`

**实际发现：**
1. **3-4 GB/s = pageable memory速度** - 证明ComfyUI没用pinned memory做这个传输！
2. 每次~135 MB = 逐层加载，不是整个模型一次传

让我修复hook，监测`torch.copy_()`:

> AGENT

找到关键了！看第1316-1317行：

```python
if (TOTAL_PINNED_MEMORY + size) > MAX_PINNED_MEMORY:
    return False  # 预算不够就不pin了！
```

问题：
1. **预算限制**: 超过108GB就不让pin了
2. **per-layer pin**: 每个layer单独pin，不是整个模型

让我hook `cudaHostRegister` 来确认实际pin了多少：

> TOOL

tool_use Write
```json
{"content": "\"\"\"\nHook for cudaHostRegister to trace pinned memory usage\n\"\"\"\n\nimport ctypes\nimport torch\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Backup original\n_cudart = torch.cuda.cudart\n_original_cudaHostRegister = _cudart.cudaHostRegister\n_original_cudaHostUnregister = _cudart.cudaHostUnregister\n\n# Stats\npin_stats = {\"pinned\": [], \"failed\": [], \"unpinned\": []}\n\ndef hooked_cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, flags):\n    result = _original_cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, flags)\n    if result == 0:\n        pin_stats[\"pinned\"].append({\"ptr\": ptr, \"size\": size})\n        print(f\"[PIN] cudaHostRegister: {size / 1e6:.1f} MB, total pinned: {sum(p['size'] for p in pin_stats['pinned']) / 1e6:.1f} MB\")\n    else:\n        pin_stats[\"failed\"].append({\"ptr\": ptr, \"size\": size})\n        print(f\"[PIN FAILED] cudaHostRegister: {size / 1e6:.1f} MB (error {result})\")\n    return result\n\ndef hooked_cudaHostUnregister(ptr):\n    result = _original_cudaHostUnregister(ptr)\n    # Find and remove from pinned\n    for i, p in enumerate(pin_stats[\"pinned\"]):\n        if p[\"ptr\"] == ptr:\n            pin_stats[\"unpinned\"].append(pin_stats[\"pinned\"].pop(i))\n            print(f\"[UNPIN] cudaHostUnregister: {p['size'] / 1e6:.1f} MB\")\n            break\n    return result\n\n# Install hooks\n_cudart.cudaHostRegister = hooked_cudaHostRegister\n_cudart.cudaHostUnregister = hooked_cudaHostUnregister\n\nprint(\"=\" * 50)\nprint(\"cudaHostRegister hook installed!\")\nprint(\"=\" * 50)\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/pin_trace_hook.py"}
```

日志很乱但信息很关键：

**关键发现：**
1. **pinned memory确实工作了** - pin了约1060 MB (每层9.4MB)
2. **但传输速度是 2.1 GB/s** - 这是pageable的速度！

这说明问题在于：**虽然pin了，但传输没用上pinned memory！**

让我看看ComfyUI实际传输用的是什么方法 - 应该是`copy_()`而不是`.to()`:

```json
{"content": "\"\"\"\nHook for tensor copy operations to trace actual H2D/D2H transfers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Backup original copy_\n_original_copy_ = torch.Tensor.copy_\n\n# Stats\nstats = defaultdict(lambda: {\"count\": 0, \"total_bytes\": 0, \"total_time\": 0})\n\ndef _hooked_copy_(self, source, non_blocking=False):\n    \"\"\"Hook for tensor.copy_()\"\"\"\n    # Determine direction\n    if self.is_cuda and source.device.type == \"cpu\":\n        direction = \"H2D\"\n    elif self.device.type == \"cpu\" and source.is_cuda:\n        direction = \"D2H\"\n    else:\n        return _original_copy_(self, source, non_blocking)\n\n    # Skip small transfers\n    bytes_size = source.nelement() * source.element_size()\n    if bytes_size < 1 * 1024 * 1024:  # < 1MB\n        return _original_copy_(self, source, non_blocking)\n\n    # Use CUDA events for accurate timing\n    start_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n    end_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n\n    start_event.record()\n    result = _original_copy_(self, source, non_blocking)\n    end_event.record()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n\n    elapsed_ms = start_event.elapsed_time(end_event)\n    bandwidth_gb = bytes_size / (elapsed_ms * 1e-3) / 1e9 if elapsed_ms > 0 else 0\n\n    # Record stats\n    stats[direction][\"count\"] += 1\n    stats[direction][\"total_bytes\"] += bytes_size\n    stats[direction][\"total_time\"] += elapsed_ms\n\n    print(f\"[{direction}_copy_] {bytes_size / 1e6:.1f} MB in {elapsed_ms:.1f}ms ({bandwidth_gb:.1f} GB/s)\")\n\n    return result\n\n# Install hook\ntorch.Tensor.copy_ = _hooked_copy_\n\nprint(\"=\" * 50)\nprint(\"copy_ hook installed!\")\nprint(\"=\" * 50)\n\ndef print_summary():\n    \"\"\"Print profiling summary\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 50)\n    print(\"copy_ Transfer Summary\")\n    print(\"=\" * 50)\n\n    for direction in [\"H2D\", \"D2H\"]:\n        s = stats[direction]\n        if s[\"count\"] > 0:\n            avg_time = s[\"total_time\"] / s[\"count\"]\n            total_gb = s[\"total_bytes\"] / 1e9\n            avg_bw = total_gb / (s[\"total_time\"] * 1e-3) if s[\"total_time\"] > 0 else 0\n            print(f\"{direction}: {s['count']} transfers, {total_gb:.2f} GB total, {avg_time:.1f}ms avg, {avg_bw:.1f} GB/s avg\")\n\n    print(\"=\" * 50)\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/copy_hook.py"}
```

```
{"text": "现在我有了完整的答案。让我给你一个详细的分析报告。\n\n---\n\n## 深入分析 ComfyUI H2D/D2H 传输机制\n\n### 1. ComfyUI 实际用什么方法做 tensor 传输？\n\n**核心传输代码位于 `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui/comfy/model_management.py`:**\n\n```python\n# cast_to 函数 (行 1202-1265)\ndef cast_to(weight, dtype=None, device=None, non_blocking=False, copy=False, stream=None, r=None):\n    ...\n    if r is None:\n        r = torch.empty_like(weight, dtype=dtype, device=device)\n    r.copy_(weight, non_blocking=non_blocking)  # 关键传输操作\n    return r\n```\n\n**底层机制：**\n- 使用 PyTorch 的 `tensor.copy_()` 方法\n- 底层调用 CUDA 的 `cudaMemcpy` (同步) 或 `cudaMemcpyAsync` (异步)\n- `non_blocking=True` 参数控制是否异步传输\n\n---\n\n### 2. pinned memory 是如何被使用的？\n\n**pinned memory 系统有两个部分：**\n\n**(a) 底层的 pin/unpin API** (`model_management.py:1295-1362`):\n```python\ndef pin_memory(tensor):\n    if torch.cuda.cudart().cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, 1) == 0:  # cudaHostRegister\n        PINNED_MEMORY[ptr] = size\n        TOTAL_PINNED_MEMORY += size\n        return True\n\ndef unpin_memory(tensor):\n    if torch.cuda.cudart().cudaHostUnregister(ptr) == 0:  # cudaHostUnregister\n        ...\n```\n\n**(b) 只有特定路径使用 pinned memory：**\n\n在 `/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui/comfy/ops.py` 中:\n\n**只有 `cast_bias_weight_with_vbar` (vbar/aimdo 优化) 使用 pinned memory:**\n```python\n# ops.py:102-137\npin = comfy.pinned_memory.get_pin(s)\nif pin is not None:\n    xfer_source = [ pin ]  # 使用已pin的内存作为源\n\n# 如果没有pin，先pin\nif signature is None and pin is None:\n    comfy.pinned_memory.pin_memory(s)  # pin内存\n    pin = comfy.pinned_memory.get_pin(s)\n\nif pin is not None:\n    # 先拷贝到 pinned buffer\n    comfy.model_management.cast_to_gathered(xfer_source, pin)\n    xfer_source = [ pin ]\n# 再从 pinned buffer 传输到 GPU\ncomfy.model_management.cast_to_gathered(xfer_source, xfer_dest, ...)\n```\n\n**标准路径 `cast_bias_weight` 不使用 pinned memory:**\n```python\n# ops.py:241-244\nweight = comfy.model_management.cast_to(s.weight, None, device, \n                                        non_blocking=non_blocking, \n                                        copy=weight_has_function, \n                                        stream=offload_stream, r=weight)\n```\n直接调用 `cast_to`，**不检查源 tensor 是否已经被 pinned**。\n\n---\n\n### 3. 为什么日志显示 pin 了但传输速度还是 pageable 的 3-4 GB/s？\n\n**根本原因：`cast_to` 函数没有利用 pinned memory 进行优化**\n\n关键问题：\n1. **pinned memory 被启用** - 日志显示 \"Enabled pinned memory\" 是因为 `MAX_PINNED_MEMORY` 被设置了\n2. **但实际传输没有使用 pinned memory** - `cast_to` 函数使用 `r.copy_(weight, non_blocking=non_blocking)` 但：\n   - 没有检查源 tensor 是否已经被 pinned\n   - 对于非 pinned 的 source tensor，即使 `non_blocking=True`，PyTorch 内部也会：\n     - 先同步地将数据拷贝到一个内部临时 pinned buffer\n     - 然后再异步传输到 GPU\n   - 这个\"先同步拷贝\"步骤导致整体传输变成同步的\n\n**PyTorch copy_ 的实际行为：**\n- Source 是 pinned memory → 使用 `cudaMemcpyAsync` (真正异步)\n- Source 是 pageable memory → 先 `cudaMemcpy` 到临时 buffer + `cudaMemcpyAsync` (整体同步)\n\n**只有以下情况会使用真正的异步传输：**\n1. 使用 vbar/aimdo 优化 (`cast_bias_weight_with_vbar`)\n2. 在 `model_patcher.py` 中手动调用 `pin_weight_to_device`\n\n---\n\n### 代码路径总结\n\n```\n标准传输路径 (不利用 pinned memory):\n  cast_bias_weight() \n    -> cast_to() \n       -> r.copy_(weight, non_blocking=True)  # 实际传输\n\n利用 pinned memory 的路径 (仅 vbar):\n  cast_bias_weight_with_vbar()\n    -> pin_memory() 或 get_pin()\n    -> cast_to_gathered(source, pin)         # 先拷贝到 pinned buffer\n    -> cast_to_gathered(pin, dest)           # 从 pinned buffer 到 GPU\n```\n\n---\n\n### 结论\n\n1. **传输方法**: `torch.Tensor.copy_()` 配合 `non_blocking=True`\n2. **pinned memory**: 有完整实现，但仅在 vbar/aimdo 优化路径中使用\n3. **速度问题原因**: 标准传输路径 (`cast_to`) 没有检查源 tensor 是否 pinned，也没有使用特殊的异步传输路径，导致实际上仍然是 pageable memory 的同步传输速度 (3-4 GB/s)\n\nagentId: a1f2114 (for resuming to continue this agent's work if needed)\n<usage>total_tokens: 56636\ntool_uses: 22\nduration_ms: 185553</usage>"}
```

总结两个agent的发现：

## 结论

### 1. ComfyUI实际传输方法
- 底层使用 `tensor.copy_()` + `non_blocking=True`
- 内部调用 `cudaMemcpyAsync`（异步）或 `cudaMemcpy`（同步）

### 2. pinned memory 为什么不工作？

**关键发现：只有 vbar/aimdo 优化路径使用 pinned memory！**

```
标准路径 (不用pinned):
  cast_bias_weight() 
    → cast_to() 
       → r.copy_(weight)  # 直接从pageable拷贝！

vbar路径 (用pinned):
  cast_bias_weight_with_vbar()
    → pin_memory() 
    → cast_to_gathered(source, pin)  # 先拷贝到pinned buffer
    → cast_to_gathered(pin, dest)   # 从pinned到GPU
```

### 3. 根本原因
`cast_to()` 函数**没有检查源tensor是否已被pinned**，直接copy，导致：
- 即使之前pin了内存，传输时还是从pageable内存拷贝
- 实际速度 = 3-4 GB/s (pageable) 而不是 20 GB/s (pinned)

```json
{"content": "\"\"\"\nHook for tensor copy operations - ONLY prints summary at the end\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\n\n# Backup original copy_\n_original_copy_ = torch.Tensor.copy_\n\n# Stats\nstats = {\"H2D\": {\"count\": 0, \"total_bytes\": 0, \"total_time\": 0},\n         \"D2H\": {\"count\": 0, \"total_bytes\": 0, \"total_time\": 0}}\n\ndef _hooked_copy_(self, source, non_blocking=False):\n    # Determine direction\n    if self.is_cuda and source.device.type == \"cpu\":\n        direction = \"H2D\"\n    elif self.device.type == \"cpu\" and source.is_cuda:\n        direction = \"D2H\"\n    else:\n        return _original_copy_(self, source, non_blocking)\n\n    bytes_size = source.nelement() * source.element_size()\n\n    # Always measure (no print)\n    start_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n    end_event = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n    start_event.record()\n    result = _original_copy_(self, source, non_blocking)\n    end_event.record()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    elapsed_ms = start_event.elapsed_time(end_event)\n\n    # Record stats only\n    stats[direction][\"count\"] += 1\n    stats[direction][\"total_bytes\"] += bytes_size\n    stats[direction][\"total_time\"] += elapsed_ms\n\n    return result\n\ntorch.Tensor.copy_ = _hooked_copy_\n\ndef print_summary():\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"H2D/D2H Transfer Summary (copy_)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"{'Direction':<10} | {'Count':>6} | {'Size (GB)':>10} | {'Avg Time':>10} | {'Avg BW':>10}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n    for direction in [\"H2D\", \"D2H\"]:\n        s = stats[direction]\n        if s[\"count\"] > 0:\n            avg_time = s[\"total_time\"] / s[\"count\"]\n            total_gb = s[\"total_bytes\"] / 1e9\n            avg_bw = total_gb / (s[\"total_time\"] * 1e-3) if s[\"total_time\"] > 0 else 0\n            print(f\"{direction:<10} | {s['count']:>6} | {total_gb:>10.2f} | {avg_time:>9.1f} ms | {avg_bw:>9.1f} GB/s\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"{direction:<10} | {'-':>6} | {'-':>10} | {'-':>10} | {'-':>10}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\ndef uninstall():\n    torch.Tensor.copy_ = _original_copy_\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/copy_hook.py"}

Record stats only\n    35→    stats[direction][\"count\"] += 1\n    36→    stats[direction][\"total_bytes\"] += bytes_size\n    37→    stats[direction][\"total_time\"] += elapsed_ms\n    38→\n    39→    return result\n    40→\n    41→torch.Tensor.copy_ = _hooked_copy_\n    42→\n    43→def print_summary():\n    44→    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    45→    print(\"H2D/D2H Transfer Summary (copy_)\")\n    46→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    47→    print(f\"{'Direction':<10} | {'Count':>6} | {'Size (GB)':>10} | {'Avg Time':>10} | {'Avg BW':>10}\")\n    48→    print(\"-\" * 60)\n    49→    for direction in [\"H2D\", \"D2H\"]:\n    50→        s = stats[direction]\n    51→        if s[\"count\"] > 0:\n    52→            avg_time = s[\"total_time\"] / s[\"count\"]\n    53→            total_gb = s[\"total_bytes\"] / 1e9\n    54→            avg_bw = total_gb / (s[\"total_time\"] * 1e-3) if s[\"total_time\"] > 0 else 0\n    55→            print(f\"{direction:<10} | {s['count']:>6} | {total_gb:>10.2f} | {avg_time:>9.1f} ms | {avg_bw:>9.1f} GB/s\")\n    56→        else:\n    57→            print(f\"{direction:<10} | {'-':>6} | {'-':>10} | {'-':>10} | {'-':>10}\")\n    58→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    59→\n    60→def uninstall():\n    61→    torch.Tensor.copy_ = _original_copy_\n    62→"}
```

```json
{"content": "\"\"\"\nHook for cudaHostRegister - NO intermediate prints, summary only\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\n\n_cudart = torch.cuda.cudart()\n_original_cudaHostRegister = _cudart.cudaHostRegister\n_original_cudaHostUnregister = _cudart.cudaHostUnregister\n\npin_stats = {\"pinned\": [], \"failed\": [], \"unpinned\": []}\n\ndef hooked_cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, flags):\n    result = _original_cudaHostRegister(ptr, size, flags)\n    if result == 0:\n        pin_stats[\"pinned\"].append({\"ptr\": ptr, \"size\": size})\n    else:\n        pin_stats[\"failed\"].append({\"ptr\": ptr, \"size\": size})\n    return result\n\ndef hooked_cudaHostUnregister(ptr):\n    result = _original_cudaHostUnregister(ptr)\n    for i, p in enumerate(pin_stats[\"pinned\"]):\n        if p[\"ptr\"] == ptr:\n            pin_stats[\"unpinned\"].append(pin_stats[\"pinned\"].pop(i))\n            break\n    return result\n\n_cudart.cudaHostRegister = hooked_cudaHostRegister\n_cudart.cudaHostUnregister = hooked_cudaHostUnregister\n\ndef print_summary():\n    total_pinned = sum(p[\"size\"] for p in pin_stats[\"pinned\"])\n    total_unpinned = sum(p[\"size\"] for p in pin_stats[\"unpinned\"])\n    total_failed = sum(p[\"size\"] for p in pin_stats[\"failed\"])\n    print(f\"Pinned:   {len(pin_stats['pinned'])} ops, {total_pinned / 1e9:.2f} GB\")\n    print(f\"Unpinned: {len(pin_stats['unpinned'])} ops, {total_unpinned / 1e9:.2f} GB\")\n    print(f\"Failed:   {len(pin_stats['failed'])} ops, {total_failed / 1e9:.2f} GB\")\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab/pin_trace_hook.py"}
```

```json
{"content": "#!/bin/bash\n# Usage: ./run_with_profiler.sh\n# After running a prompt in ComfyUI, press Ctrl+C to see profiling summary\n\ncd /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui\nexport PYTHONPATH=\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/flat_slab:$PYTHONPATH\"\n\nexec python -c \"\nimport sys\nimport signal\nimport os\n\n# 导入hooks\nfrom pin_trace_hook import print_summary as pin_summary\nfrom copy_hook import print_summary as copy_summary, uninstall\n\n# 创建信号处理器\ndef shutdown(signum, frame):\n    print('\\n\\n========== Profiling Summary ==========')\n    try:\n        uninstall()\n    except:\n        pass\n    copy_summary()\n    pin_summary()\n    print('======================================')\n    sys.exit(0)\n\nsignal.signal(signal.SIGINT, shutdown)\nsignal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, shutdown)\n\nprint('='*50)\nprint('ComfyUI + Profiling')\nprint('Run a prompt, then press Ctrl+C to see summary')\nprint('='*50)\n\nimport main\nevent_loop, _, start_all_func = main.start_comfyui()\nmain.app.logger.print_startup_warnings()\nx = start_all_func()\n\n# 使用try-except确保Ctrl+C时能打印summary\ntry:\n    event_loop.run_until_complete(x)\nexcept KeyboardInterrupt:\n    shutdown(None, None)\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f'Error: {e}')\n    shutdown(None, None)\n\"\n", "file_path": "/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/data/comfyui/run_with_profiler.sh"}
```
